Generated by `python scripts/reverse-engineering/celestial-native-intake.py`; edit the extractor or intake, not this view.

Scanned 142 authored source/document/configuration files and 36 current SDK headers/documents; extracted 12483 records.
Named address constants contain 1438 distinct numeric values; 232 also occur in the current registry. These counts do not establish function identity.

**All semantic claims remain attributed upstream claims.** Byte matches establish only equal bytes at the stated numeric RVA in our captured image. Core addresses, relocated providers and runtime patch ownership need separate evidence.

The [JSON catalogue](celestialrayone-native-inventory.json) retains complete extracted comment blocks, declarations, signatures, byte arrays, explicit witness bindings, hook rows, layout expressions and fallback numeric mentions with line ranges and source hashes.

Search locally: `python scripts/reverse-engineering/celestial-native-intake.py --query FirstPetOfType` or `--query 0x533AE0`. Query reads the versioned catalogue without needing the source cache.

Explicit W/C bindings and recognized site-table expected bytes were compared at their literal numeric RVA in the hash-verified canonical PE, except bindings in explicitly typed CoreWitness tables, which are recorded separately and not compared to the game module. Every individual result and observed byte sequence is in JSON. A difference is retained evidence, not automatically an upstream error: unresolved provider/module identity and capture state may differ.
